## 3. Critic discipline — you never grade your own work

- Every pass ends with critic rounds: a **new** agent that never saw your reasoning sees your
  frames **blind-mixed with bar frames** (same size, shuffled, neutral names; the key file is
  never shown to the critic) and answers in a fixed form.
- Read the answer; count the blind score (bar pictures it took for yours / yours it took for the
  bar) and write both numbers in the lessons.
- **Fix only the BIGGEST GAP**, at its cause, radically — one cause, one change. A BLOCKING fault
  the critic marks as a one-line fix may ride along.
- Next round = a **NEW critic**, same moments + the last report.
- **The pass is done when its exit list is true AND a critic answers `VERDICT: YES` with no
  BLOCKING fault.**
- **The same fault twice** means the fix was the wrong kind: change the method, not the amount
  (geometry instead of a texture, a real light instead of a brighter grade, another camera).
- The director's review between passes never replaces critic rounds.
*(SKILL references/critic.md)*

## 4. Hard rules

- **Made new from this voice:** cast, acting, shots, camera and set are written for this film.
  Examples teach the method and set the bar; they are never copied as material.
- **Open on a character's face**, scared or expressive, in the first frame. Never an empty scene,
  never a back.
- **Pure in t:** every frame is a function of t alone. No `Math.random` (use a seeded generator),
  no `Date`, no state between frames.
- **Never edit the engine copy** (`engine/`, `world/`, `lib/`, `specs/`). New looks, sets, props
  and characters go in `scenes/`. If an engine change seems needed, work around it and record it
  in the lessons as a gap.
- **Never touch `audio/`.** Voice lines are written as heard.
- **Anything in a place is in every shot of it.** Faces read: within about 60° of the camera, big,
  never cut by the edge.
- **PROP GUARD:** a prop that jumps on screen fails the film. `pick_up`, `put_down`, hand-overs,
  place changes on cuts, `snap` on real hits.
- **Later passes never change what earlier passes made** unless that is their job.
- Stay inside the project. Pictures only through `make_picture`; never a paid service the member
  did not choose.
*(SKILL, "Hard rules")*

## 5. The set bar — measured, and export refuses below it

`film_check { kind: 'set' }`, measured by the app: a shot needs **≥ 22 % detailed set** and
**≤ 80 % flat**; the film needs a **median shot of ≥ 28 % detail** and **≤ 78 % flat**, with
**no more than 40 % of its shots under**. Environment, Post and Shading cannot finish below it,
and **the app's export calls a film below it unfinished**. *(SKILL, "Hard rules")*

## 6. Quality has no clock

"A pass is done when its exit list is true AND a fresh critic says YES. Never cut a beat, a
character, a set piece or an effect to finish; never turn an effect down until it is gone to make
a problem disappear." Only the app's FASTER BUILDS block, when the member turned it on, speaks
about time. *(SKILL, "Quality first")* The bar is the LOOK, never the subject: any topic reaches
the same depth, light, air and acting with its own places. *(SKILL, "The bar")*
## 7. AMENDMENT — the owner's method ruling (2026-11-23 ~00:00 EET, his direct word)

After rounds 1–3 (all `free/gpt-6-luna`, blind mix) returned VERDICT: NO — rounds 2 and 3
resting on a documented blind-sort artifact (the critic sorted this film's mayor-face
frames as the bar film's character, both films sharing the house suited-character design,
then faulted the face's absence) — the owner ruled, verbatim:

> «الاتنين: ناقد جديد + فصل الفرز عن الحكم»
> ("Both: a new critic + decoupling the sort from the verdict.")

Applied from round 4 onward:

- **(a) New critic, different model class.** Each round's critic comes from a different
  model class than the round before. Round 4's critic is **Gemini** (`gemini-3.5-flash`)
  via the desk's own API connector (`custom.gemini-api`). The critic is **fresh**: no
  build history and **no prior rounds' reports or verdicts are fed to it** (this suspends
  §1's "same moments + my last report" for round 4; freshness under the ruling governs).
- **(b) The blind sort is decoupled from the verdict.** A round now runs in two separate
  fresh calls:
  - **Step A — blind sort, SCORE ONLY.** The same blind mix (our frames + bar frames,
    same size, shuffled, neutral names; key held by the driver) is sorted by the critic.
    The driver key-checks the score from the critic's per-picture lines and records it
    (header-vs-lines discrepancies recorded honestly, as in rounds 1–3). The sort is a
    number in the record — **it can no longer produce, support, or contaminate a
    verdict**: a sort slip can never again invent a missing element.
  - **Step B — the verdict, judged directly.** In a separate fresh call the critic
    receives **our frames only, labelled as the film under review** (no bar frames),
    plus the pass exit spec (§2 standing BLOCKING checks + §3.2/§3.3 for the round's
    scope), and answers in the fixed form of §5 (minus the BLIND SORT line, which now
    lives in Step A). BIGGEST GAP / BLOCKING / VERDICT rest solely on what is visibly in
    our frames measured against the spec.
- **What stands unchanged (RULES §3):** a NEW critic every round; fix only the BIGGEST
  GAP, at its cause, one cause one change; a BLOCKING one-line fix may ride along; the
  pass is done only when its exit list is true AND the critic answers `VERDICT: YES`
  with no BLOCKING fault; the same fault twice means change the method, not the amount;
  the director's review never replaces critic rounds; the driver never overturns a
  critic — a verdict resting on a factual error about the frames is recorded precisely
  and reported, not silently corrected.

NEW KINDS NEEDED
belongings_box — origin base centre; open cardboard box 0.75 w × 0.50 h × 0.55 d, rolled cloth + book corner above the rim; static geometry, transform keys only (pos); entry carries fetch:true (D1 fix of record).
gravestone — origin base centre; simple rounded-top slab 0.9 w × 1.5 h × 0.25 d, 4° back lean, no inscription dependence; static, no numeric keys.
leaf — origin centre; single oval leaf 0.28 × 0.16 with stem; keys pos (fall path) and rot (tumble); no build opts.
birds — origin perch point; flock of n=7 glyph birds (0.35 wingspan, two-triangle wings); key burst 0→1 drives per-index deterministic launch offsets (Δx ±4, Δy +3, Δz −2, closed forms of index and burst); flap = sin(2π·5·t + i) — pure in t; declare burst, n.
sea_sheet — origin sheet centre at y≈0.02 over the SEA floor; plane 120 (x) × 80 (z); key pull drives near edge z = 12 − pull (water occupies z < edge), foam line on the edge; declare pull.
wave — origin base centre at waterline; dark blue-grey breaking water wall, width w, depth 6, forward curl lip; keys h (crest height, 0→17.5) and advance (+z push); declare h, advance, w.

RATIONALE
SH13 — slow lateral track at street level, mayor leads camera 0.6 and faces 3/4 to it: the box reads in front of his chest, the three pointing mockers stay behind his shoulder near the hall door.
SH14 — gentle crane from the stone's base to above its top: gravestone holds frame centre while the far wall silhouette and the falling leaf enter on the rise; the leaf lands as the line ends.
SH15 — tight push-in on the calendar: pages fly 0→14 in 0.4 s and settle before the word "14"; the sky step-snaps to storm blue-grey on the same key.
SH16 — wide street with a full-shot sine-envelope shake (6.3/8.7 Hz, 0.26/0.18 D): poles sway, perched birds burst on اتهزت, five wide-eyed villagers rush doorways into the street.
SH17 — high-angle glide along the coast: the water edge drains 36 D seaward, both boats settle and list onto exposed sand exactly as the edge passes them.
SH18 — extreme low angle (0.65 D) on the drained sand, tilt-up to the crest: the 17.5 D water wall spans the horizon and overflows the top; the glowing measure climbs to exactly 15 before the word; four figures run past camera left to high ground.
SH19 — sepia glide from low beside the old village up the rock: young mayor's 3/4 face stays in the gaze cone the whole shot while the narrow old wave rolls over the village roofs to his left, never occluding him.

CONFLICTS
1. Pack gap — word times: audio/clean.json word timestamps are not in the brief pack. Word keys are symbolic (line:NN.wK, loader-resolved); declared numeric moment times are line-boundary-derived with uniform-word estimates, and all word-keyed states hold across the full plausible word window (see MOMENTS note). No staging is gambled on an exact word time.
2. HILL_GRAVE → "the distant silent wall": the literal COAST_WALL is 2400 D from HILL_GRAVE — a real sightline is geometrically impossible (sub-pixel at any legal fov). Staged honestly as an 18 D seawall-kind silhouette at 140 D on the hill's horizon (≈82 px at crane top). If the owner requires the literal wall, the beat cannot be staged inside the PLACES table — ruling requested.
3. Pack gap — set.js piece list: hall door, pole, calendar-wall and SEA-floor offsets are declared staging assumptions (door at village [−6,0,0.5]; calendar at [−1.5,1.7,0.25]; SEA floor reads as sand under sea_sheet). Pole sway is bound via SH16.sway to set:poles; if set poles are not keyable, Codex should bind or drop the descriptor — the اتهزت beat still reads measured via camera shake + bird burst + villager rush (all declared moments pass without it). If chunk 2's calendar entry lives in another place, this entry declares the village instance; the page channel is the law-1 declaration either way.

No other conflicts: the parked grey mayor is stowed by the named key mayor.hidden @ 'line:11.start' (stow:true); all hides sit on cuts; all key-driven build opts are declared; all fov samples lie in [32,50]; no camera enters a solid at any sampled key.

3. NEW KINDS NEEDED

carved_line: origin at center of horizontal chisel mark on rock face; size length × 0.24 × 0.08 D (default length: 3.6); reveal (0..1) unmasks the dark weathered groove + subtle chisel lip left-to-right along X.

pencil_mark: origin at center of horizontal pencil stroke on wall; size 1.4 × 0.35 × 0.04 D (graphite line + handwritten measure digits to the right); lineY offsets vertical height if needed, draw (0..1) sweeps the stroke left-to-right with a small pencil-tip/hand proxy at the leading edge while draw < 1.

concrete_pour: origin at center-bottom of the final 1 m formwork band atop the seawall; size 12.0 × 1.0 × 3.2 D (timber/steel shuttering rails + wet grey concrete fill); progress (0..1) raises the concrete fill level from 0 to 1.0 D inside the shuttering.

wave (shared with Qwen's SH18): origin at bottom-center of leading wave face (y = 0, front edge at local z = 0, body extending -Z by 24 D); size width × h × 24.0 D (default width: 70, h: 17.5); crestY drives the live water-surface peak height (0..h, capped at h = 17.5 D so SH24 reads strictly below the 18.0 D wall top), curl (0..1) drives crest-lip curl and foam cap intensity.

spray: origin at impact line center; size 26.0 × 4.5 × 3.0 D (clustered stylized cartoon white-foam puffs + vertical splash plumes); intensity (0..1) scales puff visibility/density, lift (0..3 D) drives upward vertical plume extension.

sunbeam: origin at upper cloud-break center angling down-forward toward the village; size 18.0 × 22.0 × 18.0 D (translucent warm-gold volumetric cone/shafts); opacity (0..1) drives shaft alpha.

gravestone: origin at bottom-center on ground (y = 0); size 0.95 × h × 0.40 D (default h: 1.35, rounded weathered stone stele + low plinth, facing +Z); static geometry (h optional top-level height opt).

flowers: origin at bottom-center on ground (y = 0); size 0.85 × 0.40 × 0.55 D (wrapped white/yellow tribute bouquet resting flat/angled against the gravestone plinth); static geometry.

4. RATIONALE

SH20: Close lateral track along the sepia rock face keeps carved_line_sh20 centered for الصخر while framing mayor_young at a ~34° face angle as his hand reaches the historic high-water groove.

SH21: Level, centered cutaway in ROOM holds child straight-on (0° face angle) and lands pencil_mark_sh21 at exact NDC_y = +0.062 (53.1% from bottom) at the cut to seed SH22's match-cut.

SH22: Opens on a level lock (NDC_y = +0.062) where the seawall's rising top edge (17.0 -> 18.0 D) seamlessly receives SH21's pencil line, then pulls back-and-up to reveal workers pouring the last metre.

SH23: High oblique aerial pan across NEIGHBOR_COAST shows the 17.5 D wave rising 5.5 D above the 12 D neighbour walls and engulfing the village behind on اتمسحت.

SH24: 3/4 side-profile telephoto push with pure-in-t impact shake on خبطت (staying strictly above 17.5 D and seaward of the wall) locks onto the thesis frame at حافته, where the 17.5 D water stops with a crisp 0.5 D (~120 px) dry concrete margin below the 18.0 D wall top.

SH25: Swift 0.73 s upward crane over the 18.0 D wall crest reveals the bone-dry village and villagers bathed in a breaking warm sunbeam on نجت.

SH26: Slow pull-back from the foreground gravestone and flowers reveals mocker_a/b/c bowing silently behind the grave (faces ≤ 31° to camera) with the true-scale 18.0 D wall towering on the distant coast before the hard-cut loop.

5. CONFLICTS

SH21 child rig height vs. audit body model: POS_SH21_MARK sits at y = 1.62 assuming scenes/cast/child is scaled to child height (~1.55 D). The framing covers y ∈ [0.1, 2.9] so FACE passes even if the audit uses the adult 1.9 head-centre model, but if child is unscaled (head top 2.4), raise POS_SH21_MARK.y, CAM_SH21_A/B.y, and TGT_SH21_A/B.y together by +0.88 to preserve the exact NDC_y = +0.062 match-cut with SH22.

SH22 seawall_hero merge: seawall_hero_keys_ext extends the existing hero seawall at COAST_WALL (h: 17.0 -> 18.0 in SH22, holding 18.0 thereafter) rather than spawning a second wall; the driver must merge these keys into the existing seawall entry and ensure h is declared at top level (Law 1).

SH23 NEIGHBOR_COAST wall Z-offset: set.js's exact Z offset for the existing neighbour walls inside NEIGHBOR_COAST is not quoted in the brief; SH23 stages the neighbour wall line at dz = 0 relative to PLACES.NEIGHBOR_COAST. If set.js offsets neighbour walls to dz = -16 (matching COAST_WALL), shift all SH23 Z offsets by -16 uniformly so the overtop geometry stays identical.

const F={shore:flat('#d0b58b','#9c8260'),stone:flat('#89877e','#5f615e'),sand:flat('#d7c39b','#b19b72'),water:flat('#4e91aa','#28647f'),wood:flat('#9a7047','#61452f'),roof:flat('#78533b','#50382c'),plaster:flat('#d4b58b','#a78968'),dark:flat('#403a35','#292b2d'),green:flat('#71905a','#4c6c43'),metal:flat('#59656a','#37434a'),warm:basic('#ffd18a')};
function buildWorld(){
 const g=new THREE.Group(), R=rng(1122);
 const add=(geo,mat,x,y,z,ry=0,iw=.014)=>{const m=part(geo,mat,iw);m.position.set(x,y,z);m.rotation.y=ry;g.add(m);return m;};
 const box=(mat,x,y,z,w,h,d,ry=0)=>add(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w,h,d),mat,x,y,z,ry);
 const cylinder=(mat,x,y,z,r,h)=>add(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r,r,h,10),mat,x,y,z);
 const wc=PLACES.COAST_WALL.at, vc=PLACES.COAST_VILLAGE.at, sc=PLACES.SEA.at;
 // One continuous land strip, apron at wall base, two gate practical posts.
 box(F.sand,0,-.15,-1,64,.3,40); box(F.shore,0,.02,wc[2]+2,86,.12,8); box(F.stone,0,.13,wc[2]+2,24,.24,4);
 for(const x of [-4.5,4.5]){box(F.dark,x,2.2,wc[2]+3,.38,4.4,.38);box(F.warm,x,4.2,wc[2]+3,.55,.62,.55);}
 // Village square and dense, deterministically varied small wooden houses.
 box(F.sand,vc[0],-.04,vc[2]+1,48,.08,24);
 const houses=[[-16,-5],[-10,-6],[-20,1],[-14,5],[-8,9],[8,-6],[14,-4],[20,1],[14,8],[6,10]];
 for(let i=0;i<houses.length;i++){const [dx,dz]=houses[i],x=vc[0]+dx,z=vc[2]+dz, w=3.5+R()*1.4,h=3.5+R()*1.15,d=3.1+R();
   box(F.plaster,x,h/2,z,w,h,d);add(new THREE.ConeGeometry(w*.78,1.25,4),F.roof,x,h+.62,z,Math.PI/4);box(F.dark,x-.7,1,z+d/2+.03,.7,1.8,.08);for(const wx of [-.9,.9])box(F.warm,x+wx,2.1,z+d/2+.05,.48,.55,.09);
   // yard clutter: crates, barrels, net frames and occasional cart.
   for(let j=0;j<2;j++)box(F.wood,x+(R()-.5)*4,.28,z+(R()-.5)*4,.55,.55,.55,R()*.4);
   cylinder(F.wood,x+1.5,.4,z+.5,.34,.8);
   if(i%2===0){box(F.metal,x-1.4,.55,z+2.2,1.8,.08,.1);for(let n=0;n<5;n++)box(F.dark,x-2+n*.3,.95,z+2.2,.025,.75,.025);}
   if(i%3===0){box(F.wood,x+2,.35,z+1.8,1.2,.12,.65);for(const wx of [x+1.55,x+2.45])cylinder(F.dark,wx,.16,z+1.8,.22,.18);}
 }
 // Town hall on south edge, square-facing facade and harbour edge with posts/lamps.
 const thx=vc[0],thz=vc[2]+12;box(F.plaster,thx,2.4,thz,7.2,4.8,4.8);add(new THREE.ConeGeometry(4.5,1.7,4),F.roof,thx,5.55,thz,Math.PI/4);box(F.dark,thx,1.45,thz+2.45,1.25,2.9,.12);
 for(let i=0;i<11;i++){const x=vc[0]-20+i*4;box(F.wood,x,.5,vc[2]-8,.17,1,.17);if(i%3===0){box(F.dark,x,2.05,vc[2]-8,.3,.5,.3);box(F.warm,x,2.05,vc[2]-7.8,.18,.32,.1);}}
 for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const x=vc[0]-18+i*5,z=vc[2]-9;box(F.wood,x,.2,z,.7,.4,.65);cylinder(F.dark,x+1,.34,z,.28,.68);}
 // Sea and a narrow sun-glitter ribbon; no set overlap with wall prop.
 const sea=box(F.water,sc[0],-.18,sc[2],116,.16,52);sea.userData.shimmer=true;
 box(F.water,0,-.08,-67,118,.08,3); // distant horizon strip
 // Distant place pads and one recognizable silhouette per place.
 const nc=PLACES.NEIGHBOR_COAST.at;box(F.sand,nc[0],-.12,nc[2],100,.24,48);box(F.stone,nc[0],6,nc[2]-10,24,12,.8);for(const x of [nc[0]-20,nc[0],nc[0]+20]){box(F.plaster,x,2.1,nc[2]+8,4,4.2,3.5);add(new THREE.ConeGeometry(2.8,1,4),F.roof,x,4.7,nc[2]+8);}
 const rc=PLACES.FLASHBACK_ROCK.at;box(F.sand,rc[0],-.12,rc[2],78,.24,48);add(new THREE.DodecahedronGeometry(9,0),F.stone,rc[0],5,rc[2]-5,0,.03);
 const ic=PLACES.ROOM.at;box(F.wood,ic[0],1.5,ic[2]-3,8,3, .35);box(F.plaster,ic[0],1.5,ic[2]+3,8,3,.35);
 const hc=PLACES.HILL_GRAVE.at;add(new THREE.SphereGeometry(19,20,10),F.green,hc[0],-5,hc[2],0,0);box(F.stone,hc[0],.8,hc[2],1.1,1.6,.35);
 g.traverse(m=>{m.frustumCulled=false;});return {group:g,sea};
}
export async function buildSet(THREEArg,scene,opts={},kit){
 const rig=createLightRig(THREE,PLACES), look=opts.post?createPost(THREE,rig,opts.post):undefined;
 if(look&&opts.post.shade)look.shading=createRigShade(THREE,rig,opts.post.shade);
 const {group,sea}=buildWorld();scene.add(group);
 const wall=PLACES.COAST_WALL.at,v=PLACES.COAST_VILLAGE.at,s=PLACES.SEA.at;
 const marks={wall_base:[PLACES.COAST_WALL.at[0],PLACES.COAST_WALL.at[2]],gate:[PLACES.COAST_WALL.at[0],PLACES.COAST_WALL.at[2]],square:[v[0],v[2]],townhall:[v[0],v[2]+12],harbour:[v[0],v[2]-8],boat_anchor:[s[0],s[2]+14]};
 return {group,marks,points:marks,rig,look,update(t){rig.update(t);sea.position.y=-.18+Math.sin(t*1.4)*.008;}};
}
// Static staging audit for Fudai Wall, chunk 2. Pure Node; no renderer/deps.
// Geometry uses calibrated head centre y=1.9, r=.5, hip=1.35, top=2.4: phase-6
// engine measurement was ~1.85–1.95; F0 (target 1.55, dist 5.1, fov 34) passes
// and F687 (dist 2.9, fov 30) crops, consistent with this model and not y=1.55.
// Named assumptions: stage-dependent lowCams rests are unavailable, so the
// documented calibrated rest rule below is used. Camera roll is ignored (SH07
// is ≤5°). Face-limit clamping from the renderer is not applied. Box frustum
// corner counts use a world AABB after yaw rotation, per the audit specification.
// Phase-11 F570 face-staging amendment (Codex seat): see RUN_RECORD phase 11.
// D2-CLEAR solids are copied from tools/audit_d1_d2.mjs (town hall, ten village
// houses, three neighbour houses); wall and gateleaf are additional occluders.
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import film from '../scenes/film.js';
import { resolver, resolveTimes, autoMove } from '../lib/film/resolve.js';
import { easeFn } from '../engine/ease.js';
import { lowCams } from '../lib/film/end.js';
import { hh } from '../engine/clips.js';

const ROOT = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const words = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'audio/clean.json'), 'utf8'));
const lines = [...new Set(words.map(w => w.line))].map(line => {
  const ws = words.filter(w => w.line === line);
  return { line, start: ws[0].start, end: ws.at(-1).end };
});
const voice = { words, lines };
const R = resolver(voice);
let shots = resolveTimes(film.shots, R, 'shots');
const acts = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(film.acts || {}).map(([id, a]) => [id, resolveTimes(a, R, `acts.${id}`)]));
const props = resolveTimes(film.props || [], R, 'props');
const dur = 68.08;
const cast = film.cast;

// Reconstruct the loader's character/rest inputs from each cast spec. These
// specs share the renderer's proportion fields; hip and head heights stay tied
// to the character's leg, neck, and head dimensions.
const characters = Object.entries(cast).map(([name, c]) => {
  const raw = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'scenes', c.spec), 'utf8'));
  const spec = { ...raw, id: name };
  const legLen = spec.limbs?.legLen ?? 1.35;
  const hipY = legLen * .93;
  const armLen = spec.limbs?.armLen ?? .95;
  const rest = { hipY, headY: hipY + (spec.body?.neckHeight ?? .35) + (spec.body?.neckToHead ?? .35), neckToHead: spec.body?.neckToHead ?? .35, legLen, armLen,
    ankleY: (spec.limbs?.width ?? .058) / 2 + .005, hipR: [-.22, -.2, 0], hipL: [.22, -.2, 0],
    shoulderR: [-.4, .4, 0], shoulderL: [.4, .4, 0], neck: [0, .4, 0] };
  const timeline = resolveTimes(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(acts[name] || {})), R, `acts.${name}`);
  timeline.root = timeline.root || { x: c.at?.[0] ?? 0, z: c.at?.[1] ?? 0, ry: c.ry ?? 0 };
  timeline.settle ??= true;
  return { name, spec, timeline, rest };
});
const rests = Object.fromEntries(characters.map(ch => [ch.name, ch.rest]));

function posAt(id, t) {
  const c = cast[id], base = c.at, walks = (acts[id]?.walk || []).filter(w => t >= w.t0).sort((a,b)=>a.t0-b.t0);
  if (!walks.length) return [base[0], 0, base[1]];
  const w = walks.at(-1), path = w.path, speed = w.speed || 1;
  let remain = Math.max(0, t - w.t0) * speed;
  for (let i=1;i<path.length;i++) {
    const a=path[i-1], b=path[i], d=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);
    if (remain <= d) { const u=d ? remain/d:1; return [a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*u,0,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*u]; }
    remain -= d;
  }
  const p=path.at(-1); return [p[0],0,p[1]];
}
function faceAt(id,t,p) {
  const a=acts[id]||{}, walks=(a.walk||[]).filter(w=>t>=w.t0);
  if(walks.length){const w=walks.at(-1), path=w.path;let rem=Math.max(0,t-w.t0)*(w.speed||1);
    for(let i=1;i<path.length;i++){const d=Math.hypot(path[i][0]-path[i-1][0],path[i][1]-path[i-1][1]);if(rem<=d)return [path[i][0]-path[i-1][0],path[i][1]-path[i-1][1]];rem-=d;}
    const n=path.length;return [path[n-1][0]-path[n-2][0],path[n-1][1]-path[n-2][1]]; }
  const look=(a.look||[]).filter(k=>k.t<=t).sort((x,y)=>x.t-y.t).at(-1);
  if(look)return [look.at[0]-p[0],look.at[2]-p[2]];
  const ry=(cast[id].ry||0)*Math.PI/180;return [Math.sin(ry),Math.cos(ry)];
}
const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const lerp=(a,b,u)=>a+(b-a)*u;
function lerpV(a,b,u){return a.map((v,i)=>lerp(v,b[i],u));}
const raises=[];
const lowered = lowCams(shots, characters, rests, { log: msg => raises.push(msg) });
shots = lowered.shots;
const moved=await autoMove(shots,dur);
const shotById=id=>moved.find(s=>s.id===id);

function camAt(shot,t){const c=shot.camera,a=c.t0??shot.t0,b=c.t1??shot.t1;let u=clamp((t-a)/(b-a));u=easeFn(c.ease??'outCubic')(u);return {pos:lerpV(c.from.pos,c.to.pos,u),target:lerpV(c.from.target,c.to.target,u),fov:lerp(c.from.fov??26,c.to.fov??26,u)};}

function add(a,b){return a.map((x,i)=>x+b[i]);}
function ry(v,yaw){const a=yaw*Math.PI/180;return [v[0]*Math.cos(a)+v[2]*Math.sin(a),v[1],-v[0]*Math.sin(a)+v[2]*Math.cos(a)];}
function box(center,size,yaw=0){const [x,y,z]=size,a=Math.abs(yaw*Math.PI/180);const ex=Math.abs(x*Math.cos(a))+Math.abs(z*Math.sin(a)),ez=Math.abs(x*Math.sin(a))+Math.abs(z*Math.cos(a));return {center,min:[center[0]-ex/2,center[1]-y/2,center[2]-ez/2],max:[center[0]+ex/2,center[1]+y/2,center[2]+ez/2],size:[ex,y,ez]};}
function corners(b){const out=[];for(const x of [b.min[0],b.max[0]])for(const y of [b.min[1],b.max[1]])for(const z of [b.min[2],b.max[2]])out.push([x,y,z]);return out;}
function makeFrame(cam){const f=norm(sub(cam.target,cam.pos)), right=norm(cross(f,[0,1,0])), up=norm(cross(right,f));const v=cam.fov*Math.PI/180,h=2*Math.atan(Math.tan(v/2)*1080/1920);const planes=[];
  for(const [axis,ang,other,swap] of [[right,h/2,up,false],[neg(right),h/2,up,true],[up,v/2,right,true],[neg(up),v/2,right,false]]){const edge=norm(add(f,mul(axis,Math.tan(ang))));let n=norm(swap?cross(other,edge):cross(edge,other));if(dot(n,f)<0)n=neg(n);planes.push({n});}
  planes.push({n:f,dot:0,origin:cam.pos,near:true},{n:neg(f),dot:0,origin:cam.pos,far:true});
  return {cam,planes,point(p){const d=sub(p,cam.pos);return dot(d,f)>=.1&&dot(d,f)<=2000&&planes.slice(0,4).every(pl=>dot(d,pl.n)>=-1e-7);},sphere(p,r){const d=sub(p,cam.pos);return dot(d,f)-r>=.1&&dot(d,f)+r<=2000&&planes.slice(0,4).every(pl=>dot(d,pl.n)>=r-1e-7);},depth(p){return dot(sub(p,cam.pos),f);},height(size,p){const d=this.depth(p);return d>0?2*Math.atan(size/(2*d))/(cam.fov*Math.PI/180)*1920:null;},width(size,p){const d=this.depth(p);return d>0?2*Math.atan(size/(2*d))/(h)*1080:null;},screen(p){const d=sub(p,cam.pos),z=dot(d,f);if(z<=.1)return null;const x=dot(d,right)/(z*Math.tan(h/2)),y=dot(d,up)/(z*Math.tan(v/2));return [(x+1)*540,(1-y)*960];}};
}
const sub=(a,b)=>a.map((v,i)=>v-b[i]), mul=(a,k)=>a.map(v=>v*k), dot=(a,b)=>a.reduce((s,v,i)=>s+v*b[i],0), norm=a=>mul(a,1/(Math.hypot(...a)||1)), cross=(a,b)=>[a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0]], neg=a=>mul(a,-1);
function segBox(p0,p1,b){let lo=0,hi=1;for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const d=p1[i]-p0[i];if(Math.abs(d)<1e-9){if(p0[i]<b.min[i]||p0[i]>b.max[i])return null;}else{let a=(b.min[i]-p0[i])/d,z=(b.max[i]-p0[i])/d;if(a>z)[a,z]=[z,a];lo=Math.max(lo,a);hi=Math.min(hi,z);if(lo>hi)return null;}}return lo>1e-6&&lo<1-1e-6?lo:null;}
function occluder(p0,p1,t,exclude){return occs(t).filter(o=>o.name!==exclude).map(o=>({name:o.name,k:segBox(p0,p1,o)})).filter(x=>x.k!=null).sort((a,b)=>a.k-b.k)[0]?.name||null;}
const solids=[{name:'town hall',min:[-4.5,0,9.5],max:[4.5,6.4,18.5]}];
for(const [x,z] of [[-16,-3],[-10,-4],[-20,3],[-14,7],[-8,11],[8,-4],[14,-2],[20,3],[14,10],[6,12]])solids.push({name:`village house @${x},${z}`,min:[x-2.45,0,z-2.05],max:[x+2.45,6,z+2.05]});
for(const x of [580,600,620])solids.push({name:`neighbour house @${x}`,min:[x-2.45,0,8-2.05],max:[x+2.45,5.6,8+2.05]});
function occs(t){const out=solids.map(b=>({...b}));out.push({name:'seawall',...box([0,9,-16],[90,18,1.15])});const g=props.find(p=>p.id==='gateleaf');if(g){const st=propState(g,t);if(st.visible)out.push({name:'gateleaf',...box(add(st.at,[0,17.88*(st.lift-.5),0]),[7,17.88,.36])});}return out;}
function propState(p,t){const ks=p.keys||[], before=ks.filter(k=>k.t<=t).sort((a,b)=>a.t-b.t), key=before.at(-1)||{};const lastField=f=>before.filter(k=>k[f]!=null).at(-1)?.[f];const visible=key.drop?true:(lastField('in')??p.in??'world')==='world';const at=[...(lastField('at')??p.at)],rot=[...(lastField('rot')??p.rot??[0,0,0])];
  const val=(field,def)=>{if(p[field]==null)return def;const keyed=before.filter(k=>k[field]!=null);if(!keyed.length)return p[field];const prev=keyed.at(-1),ix=keyed.length-1,ease=prev.ease;if(ease!=null&&ix>0){const prior=keyed[ix-1],u=clamp((t-prev.t)/ease);const sm=u*u*(3-2*u);return lerp(prior[field],prev[field],sm);}return prev[field];};
  return {visible,at,rot,page:val('page',0),h:val('h',.8),lift:val('lift',0),lineY:val('lineY',1),pin:val('pin',0),cx:val('cx',0),cy:val('cy',12)};}
function dropState(p,t){const k=(p.keys||[]).filter(k=>k.t<=t).at(-1);if(!k?.drop)return null;const d=k.drop,dt=t-k.t,g=d.g??9.8,v0=d.v0||[0,0,0],from=d.from||k.at||p.at,ground=d.ground??0,a=g/2,c=from[1]-ground,T=a>0?(v0[1]+Math.sqrt(v0[1]**2+4*a*Math.max(0,c)))/(2*a):Infinity;if(d.hide&&dt>T)return null;const u=Math.min(dt,T),y=from[1]+v0[1]*u-.5*g*u*u;return [from[0]+v0[0]*u,y,from[2]+v0[2]*u];}
function boxCheck(frame,b,minPx=0,skipOcc=false,name='subject'){const n=corners(b).filter(p=>frame.point(p)).length,center=frame.point(b.center),px=frame.height(b.size[1],b.center),pw=frame.width(b.size[0],b.center),by=!skipOcc?occluder(frame.cam.pos,b.center,currentTime,name):null,behind=frame.depth(b.center)<=0;return {pass:center&&!by&&px!=null&&px>=minPx&&(n>=4||px>=1920||pw>=1080),n,px,pw,center,occluded:by,behind};}
let currentTime=0;
function propBox(id,t){const p=props.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!p)return null;const s=propState(p,t);if(!s.visible)return null;const a=s.at,r=s.rot[1]||0,kind=p.kind;let B,points=[];
  if(kind==='boardsign')B=box(add(a,[0,(p.post?p.leg||1.2:0)+(p.h||.8)/2,0]),[p.w||2,p.h||.8,.12],r);
  if(kind==='calendar')B=box(add(a,[0,1.9,0]),[2.2,3.5,.2],r);
  if(kind==='measure'){if(id.endsWith('label'))return null;}
  if(kind==='money')B=box(add(a,[0,.55,0]),[3.7,1.15,2.1],r);
  if(kind==='crane')B=box(add(a,ry([2,8,0],r)),[11.5,16.8,1.2],r);
  if(kind==='gateleaf')B=box(add(a,[0,17.88*(s.lift-.5),0]),[7,17.88,.36],r);
  if(kind==='seawall')B=box(add(a,[0,9,0]),[90,18,1.15],r);
  if(kind==='blueprint')B=box(add(a,[0,1.7,0]),[3.4,3.4,.2],r);
  if(kind==='orb')return {sphere:add(a,[s.cx,s.cy,0]),r:p.r||1.5,state:s};
  if(kind==='coins'){const q=dropState(p,t);return q?{box:box(q,[.5,.5,.5]),state:s}:null;}
  return B?{box:B,state:s}:null;
}
const houseBoxes=[580,600,620].map(x=>box([x,2.1,8],[4,4.2,3.5]));
const wallBox=box([600,6,-10],[24,12,.8]);
const moments=[
 [219,7.3,'SH04'],[241,8.0333333333,'SH04'],[288,9.6,'SH05'],[345,11.5,'SH05'],[414,13.8,'SH05'],[474,15.8,'SH06'],[537,17.9,'SH07'],[570,19,'SH07'],[590,19.6666667,'SH07B'],[621,20.7,'SH08'],[639,21.3,'SH08'],[669,22.3,'SH09'],[687,22.9,'SH09'],[711,23.7,'SH10'],[723,24.1,'SH10'],[768,25.6,'SH11'],[798,26.6,'SH11'],[822,27.4,'SH11'],[870,29,'SH12']
];
function pointCheck(frame,p){return frame.point(p)&&!occluder(frame.cam.pos,p,currentTime);}
function screenVerticalSpan(frame, points){const ys=points.map(p=>frame.screen(p)).filter(p=>p!==null).map(p=>p[1]);return ys.length?Math.max(...ys)-Math.min(...ys):0;}
function faceCheck(frame,id,t,minPx=0){const p=posAt(id,t),head=[p[0],1.9,p[2]],f=faceAt(id,t,p),to=[frame.cam.pos[0]-p[0],frame.cam.pos[2]-p[2]],angle=Math.acos(clamp(dot(norm(f),norm(to)),-1,1))*180/Math.PI;const visible=frame.sphere(head,.5),occ=occluder(frame.cam.pos,head,t),px=frame.height(1,head);return {pass:angle<=60&&visible&&!occ&&px!=null&&px>=minPx,angle,visible,occ,px};}
const results=[];
for(const [frameNo,t,shotId] of moments){currentTime=t;const shot=shotById(shotId);if(!shot){results.push({frameNo,shotId,checks:['missing shot']});continue;}const cam=camAt(shot,t),fr=makeFrame(cam),checks=[];let occupancy=false;
 if(cam.fov<24||cam.fov>50)checks.push(`fov ${cam.fov.toFixed(2)} out of grammar [24, 50]`);
 const inside=occs(t).find(o=>cam.pos.every((v,i)=>v>=o.min[i]-.3&&v<=o.max[i]+.3));

 const prop=(id,minPx,component)=>{let q=propBox(id,t);if(id==='measure_plate'){const p=props.find(x=>x.id==='measure'),s=propState(p,t);q={box:box(add(s.at,[.32,p.full??18,0]),[1.35,.42,.1],s.rot[1]||0),state:s};}
  if(id==='seawall_gate'){const p=props.find(x=>x.id==='seawall'),s=propState(p,t);q={box:box(add(s.at,[0,8.94,.62]),[7,17.88,.55]),state:s};}
  if(!q){checks.push(`${id} absent`);return false;}let ck;if(q.sphere){const inside=fr.sphere(q.sphere,q.r),oc=occluder(cam.pos,q.sphere,t,id),px=fr.height(q.r*2,q.sphere);ck={pass:inside&&!oc&&px!=null&&px>=minPx,px,inside,oc,behind:fr.depth(q.sphere)<=0};}
  else {ck=boxCheck(fr,q.box,minPx,false,id);}
  const by=ck.occluded||ck.oc;checks.push(`${id} ${ck.pass?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${ck.px?.toFixed(0)??'n/a'}px${ck.behind?' behind camera':''} corners ${ck.n??'-'}/8${by?` occluded by ${by}`:''}`);if(ck.center&&ck.px>=230)occupancy=true;return ck.pass;};
 const face=(id,minPx=0)=>{const f=faceCheck(fr,id,t,minPx),behind=fr.depth([posAt(id,t)[0],1.9,posAt(id,t)[2]])<=0;checks.push(`${id} face ${f.pass?'PASS':'FAIL'} angle ${f.angle.toFixed(1)}° head ${behind?'behind camera':f.visible?'in':'out'} ${f.px?.toFixed(0)??'n/a'}px${f.occ?` occluded by ${f.occ}`:''}`);if(f.visible&&f.px>=230)occupancy=true;return f.pass;};
 const body=(id,b)=>{const ck=boxCheck(fr,b,0,true,id);return ck.pass;};
 const bodyIds=ids=>ids.filter(id=>{const p=posAt(id,t),b=box([p[0],1,p[2]],[.9,2,.5]);return body(id,b);});
 const point=(name,p)=>{const pass=pointCheck(fr,p),behind=fr.depth(p)<=0;checks.push(`${name} ${pass?'PASS':'FAIL'} point ${behind?'behind camera':pass?'in':'out'} frustum`);return pass;};
 switch(frameNo){
 case 219: face('mayor');{const n=bodyIds(['villager_1','villager_2','villager_3','villager_4','villager_5','villager_6','mocker_a','mocker_b','mocker_c']).length;checks.push(`BODY ${n>=2?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${n}/2`);}break;
 case 241: face('mayor');prop('popsign',60);{const n=bodyIds(['villager_1','villager_2','villager_3','villager_4','villager_5','villager_6','mocker_a','mocker_b','mocker_c']).length;checks.push(`BODY ${n>=2?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${n}/2`);}break;

 case 474: {const c=boxCheck(fr,wallBox,150,true,'neighbour wall');checks.push(`neighbour wall ${c.pass?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${c.px?.toFixed(0)??'n/a'}px corners ${c.n}/8`);if(c.center&&c.px>=230)occupancy=true;prop('sticker_a',60);prop('sticker_b',60);const n=houseBoxes.filter(b=>boxCheck(fr,b,0,true).pass).length;checks.push(`neighbour houses ${n>=1?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${n}/1`);break;}
 case 537: face('mocker_a',200);break;

// tools/audit_d1_d2.mjs — Seat 1 driver-side static audits for the Fudai build.
// D1: film.props schema (ARRAY of {id, kind, in:'world', at:[x,y,z], keys:[{t, at}]},
//     ids unique, keys non-empty; box entry keeps fetch:true when present),
//     purity scan (no Math.random / no Date in scene sources).
// D2: ONE PLACES table exported from scenes/set.js (all 7 places, at + bounds);
//     every prop `at`, cast `at`, and camera target falls inside a declared
//     place's bounds (zone audit); camera targets sane in y (frustum pre-audit —
//     the looked-at zoom frustum check stays Seat 4's, on the probe stills).
// Run: node tools/audit_d1_d2.mjs   (from the project folder)
// Exit 0 = PASS, 1 = FAIL (failures printed, one per line).
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';

const ROOT = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const fails = [];
const notes = [];
const ok = (cond, msg) => { if (!cond) fails.push(msg); };

const filmSrc = readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'scenes/film.js'), 'utf8');
const setSrc = readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'scenes/set.js'), 'utf8');

// ---- purity (RULES §4) across all authored scene sources ----
import { readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
const scan = (dir) => {
  for (const e of readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const p = join(dir, e.name);
    if (e.isDirectory()) scan(p);
    else if (e.name.endsWith('.js')) {
      const s = readFileSync(p, 'utf8');
      if (/Math\.random/.test(s)) fails.push(`PURITY: Math.random in ${p}`);
      if (/\bDate\s*\(|Date\.now/.test(s)) fails.push(`PURITY: Date in ${p}`);
    }
  }
};
scan(join(ROOT, 'scenes'));

// ---- load the film + the PLACES table ----
const film = (await import(join(ROOT, 'scenes/film.js'))).default;
const setMod = await import(join(ROOT, 'scenes/set.js'));
const PLACES = setMod.PLACES;
ok(PLACES && typeof PLACES === 'object', 'D2: scenes/set.js does not export a PLACES table');
ok(/import\s*\{[^}]*PLACES[^}]*\}\s*from\s*'\.\/set\.js'/.test(filmSrc),
  'D2: film.js does not import PLACES from ./set.js');

const EXPECTED = ['COAST_WALL', 'COAST_VILLAGE', 'SEA', 'NEIGHBOR_COAST', 'FLASHBACK_ROCK', 'ROOM', 'HILL_GRAVE'];
for (const name of EXPECTED) {
  const p = PLACES?.[name];
  ok(p, `D2: PLACES.${name} missing`);
  if (!p) continue;
  ok(Array.isArray(p.at) && p.at.length === 3, `D2: PLACES.${name}.at is not a 3-vector`);
  ok(p.bounds && Array.isArray(p.bounds.x) && Array.isArray(p.bounds.z),
    `D2: PLACES.${name}.bounds missing {x:[..], z:[..]}`);
}
const inBounds = (x, z) => EXPECTED.some(n => {
  const b = PLACES[n]?.bounds; if (!b) return false;
  return x >= b.x[0] && x <= b.x[1] && z >= b.z[0] && z <= b.z[1];
});
const placeOf = (x, z) => EXPECTED.find(n => {
  const b = PLACES[n]?.bounds; if (!b) return false;
  return x >= b.x[0] && x <= b.x[1] && z >= b.z[0] && z <= b.z[1];
});

// ---- D1: props schema ----
ok(Array.isArray(film.props), 'D1: film.props is NOT an array');
if (Array.isArray(film.props)) {
  const ids = new Set();
  for (const p of film.props) {
    ok(p.id && !ids.has(p.id), `D1: prop id missing/duplicated: ${p.id}`);
    ids.add(p.id);
    ok(typeof p.kind === 'string' && /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(p.kind), `D1: prop ${p.id}: bad kind ${p.kind}`);
    ok(p.in === 'world', `D1: prop ${p.id}: in !== 'world' (${p.in})`);
    ok(Array.isArray(p.at) && p.at.length === 3 && p.at.every(v => Number.isFinite(v)),
      `D1: prop ${p.id}: at is not a finite 3-vector`);
    ok(Array.isArray(p.keys) && p.keys.length > 0, `D1: prop ${p.id}: keys empty`);
    if (Array.isArray(p.keys)) {
      const nums = p.keys.map(k => k.t).filter(t => typeof t === 'number');
      ok(nums.every((v, i) => i === 0 || v >= nums[i - 1]), `D1: prop ${p.id}: numeric keys not sorted by t`);
      for (const k of p.keys) if (k.at) ok(Array.isArray(k.at) && k.at.length === 3, `D1: prop ${p.id}: a key's at is not a 3-vector`);
    }
    // D2 zone: prop position inside a declared place
    if (Array.isArray(p.at)) ok(inBounds(p.at[0], p.at[2]),
      `D2: prop ${p.id} at [${p.at}] is outside every place's bounds`);
    else notes.push(`prop ${p.id}: no at to zone-check`);
    if (p.id === 'box' || p.kind === 'box') ok(p.fetch === true, 'D1: belongings box entry lost fetch: true');
  }
  if (!film.props.some(p => p.id === 'box' || p.kind === 'box'))
    notes.push('D1: no belongings-box prop yet (expected at opening stage; fetch:true check arms when it lands)');
}

// ---- D2: cast + cameras inside places ----
for (const [id, c] of Object.entries(film.cast || {})) {
  ok(Array.isArray(c.at) && c.at.length === 2, `D2: cast ${id}: at is not [x, z]`);
  if (Array.isArray(c.at)) ok(inBounds(c.at[0], c.at[1]),
    `D2: cast ${id} at [${c.at}] is outside every place's bounds`);
}
ok(Array.isArray(film.shots) && film.shots.length > 0, 'D2: film.shots empty');
for (const [i, s] of (film.shots || []).entries()) {
  const ends = s.camera?.move === 'park' ? [s.camera] : [s.camera?.from, s.camera?.to];
  for (const e of ends) {
    if (!e) { fails.push(`D2: shot ${i + 1}: missing camera from/to`); continue; }
    ok(Array.isArray(e.target) && e.target.length === 3, `D2: shot ${i + 1}: target not a 3-vector`);
    if (Array.isArray(e.target)) {
      ok(inBounds(e.target[0], e.target[2]),
        `D2: shot ${i + 1}: target [${e.target}] outside every place's bounds`);
      ok(e.target[1] >= 0 && e.target[1] <= 19.5,
        `D2: shot ${i + 1}: target y ${e.target[1]} outside sane 0..19.5 (wall is 18 D)`);
    }
    ok(Array.isArray(e.pos) && e.pos.length === 3, `D2: shot ${i + 1}: pos not a 3-vector`);
  }
}

// ---- D2-CLEAR: camera paths vs building solids (phase-7 extension) ----
// Conservative solids from set.js buildWorld at MAXIMUM dimensions (village house
// w<=4.9 -> hx 2.45, d<=4.1 -> hz 2.05, h<=4.65 + roof cone 1.25 -> top 6.0; town hall
// box + cone r4.5 -> x +-4.5, z 9.5..18.5, top 6.4; neighbour houses h 4.2 + roof -> 5.6).
// A path clearing these boxes clears the real buildings. Wall/storeys are NOT solids.
const SOLIDS = [{ name: 'town hall', min: [-4.5, 0, 9.5], max: [4.5, 6.4, 18.5] }];
for (const [hx, hz] of [[-16, -3], [-10, -4], [-20, 3], [-14, 7], [-8, 11], [8, -4], [14, -2], [20, 3], [14, 10], [6, 12]])
  SOLIDS.push({ name: `village house @${hx},${hz}`, min: [hx - 2.45, 0, hz - 2.05], max: [hx + 2.45, 6.0, hz + 2.05] });
for (const hx of [580, 600, 620])
  SOLIDS.push({ name: `neighbour house @${hx}`, min: [hx - 2.45, 0, 8 - 2.05], max: [hx + 2.45, 5.6, 8 + 2.05] });
function segHitsBox(p0, p1, b) {
  let t0 = 0, t1 = 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const d = p1[i] - p0[i];
    if (Math.abs(d) < 1e-9) { if (p0[i] < b.min[i] || p0[i] > b.max[i]) return false; }
    else {
      let ta = (b.min[i] - p0[i]) / d, tb = (b.max[i] - p0[i]) / d;
      if (ta > tb) { const t = ta; ta = tb; tb = t; }
      t0 = Math.max(t0, ta); t1 = Math.min(t1, tb);
      if (t0 > t1) return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}
// Scope: chunk-2 shots (index >= 3, SH04+). SH01-SH03 are frozen and critic-passed;
// their paths were fleet-probe-verified in phases 1-3, and the max-dim box overstates
// the town-hall cone (round cross-section), so the opening is exempt from this check.
for (const [i, s] of (film.shots || []).entries()) {
  if (i < 3) continue;
  const ends = s.camera?.move === 'park' ? [s.camera] : [s.camera?.from, s.camera?.to];
  const p0 = ends[0]?.pos, p1 = ends[ends.length - 1]?.pos;
  if (!Array.isArray(p0) || !Array.isArray(p1)) continue;
  for (const b of SOLIDS)
    ok(!segHitsBox(p0, p1, b), `D2-CLEAR: shot ${i + 1} (${s.id || '?'}) camera path intersects ${b.name}`);
}
notes.push(`D2-CLEAR: ${SOLIDS.length} building solids checked per shot`);

// ---- format locks ----
ok(film.fps === 30, `FORMAT: fps !== 30 (${film.fps})`);
ok(JSON.stringify(film.size) === '[1080,1920]', `FORMAT: size !== [1080,1920] (${film.size})`);
ok(film.voice?.file === 'voice_owner.wav', `FORMAT: voice.file !== voice_owner.wav (${film.voice?.file})`);
ok(Array.isArray(film.voice?.lines) && film.voice.lines.length === 24,
  `FORMAT: voice.lines !== 24 (${film.voice?.lines?.length})`);

console.log('— D1/D2 AUDIT —');
for (const n of notes) console.log('note:', n);
if (fails.length) { console.log(`FAIL (${fails.length}):`); for (const f of fails) console.log('  ✗', f); process.exit(1); }
console.log('PASS: D1 schema + purity, D2 zones/frustum pre-audit, format locks.');
console.log('places:', EXPECTED.map(n => `${n}@${PLACES[n].at}`).join(' · '));

// Static staging audit for Fudai Wall, chunk 2. Pure Node; no renderer/deps.
// Geometry uses calibrated head centre y=1.9, r=.5, hip=1.35, top=2.4: phase-6
// engine measurement was ~1.85–1.95; F0 (target 1.55, dist 5.1, fov 34) passes
// and F687 (dist 2.9, fov 30) crops, consistent with this model and not y=1.55.

// documented calibrated rest rule below is used. Camera roll is ignored (SH07
// is ≤5°). Face-limit clamping from the renderer is not applied. Box frustum
// corner counts use a world AABB after yaw rotation, per the audit specification.
// Phase-11 F570 face-staging amendment (Codex seat): see RUN_RECORD phase 11.
// D2-CLEAR solids are copied from tools/audit_d1_d2.mjs (town hall, ten village
// houses, three neighbour houses); wall and gateleaf are additional occluders.

  return { line, start: ws[0].start, end: ws.at(-1).end };
});

const dur = 68.08;

// Reconstruct the loader's character/rest inputs from each cast spec. These
// specs share the renderer's proportion fields; hip and head heights stay tied
// to the character's leg, neck, and head dimensions.
const characters = Object.entries(cast).map(([name, c]) => {
  const raw = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'scenes', c.spec), 'utf8'));
  const spec = { ...raw, id: name };
  const legLen = spec.limbs?.legLen ?? 1.35;
  const hipY = legLen * .93;
  const armLen = spec.limbs?.armLen ?? .95;
  const rest = { hipY, headY: hipY + (spec.body?.neckHeight ?? .35) + (spec.body?.neckToHead ?? .35), neckToHead: spec.body?.neckToHead ?? .35, legLen, armLen,
    ankleY: (spec.limbs?.width ?? .058) / 2 + .005, hipR: [-.22, -.2, 0], hipL: [.22, -.2, 0],
    shoulderR: [-.4, .4, 0], shoulderL: [.4, .4, 0], neck: [0, .4, 0] };
  const timeline = resolveTimes(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(acts[name] || {})), R, `acts.${name}`);
  timeline.root = timeline.root || { x: c.at?.[0] ?? 0, z: c.at?.[1] ?? 0, ry: c.ry ?? 0 };
  timeline.settle ??= true;
  return { name, spec, timeline, rest };
});
const rests = Object.fromEntries(characters.map(ch => [ch.name, ch.rest]));

function posAt(id, t) {
  const c = cast[id], base = c.at, walks = (acts[id]?.walk || []).filter(w => t >= w.t0).sort((a,b)=>a.t0-b.t0);
  if (!walks.length) return [base[0], 0, base[1]];
  const w = walks.at(-1), path = w.path, speed = w.speed || 1;
  let remain = Math.max(0, t - w.t0) * speed;
  for (let i=1;i<path.length;i++) {
    const a=path[i-1], b=path[i], d=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);
    if (remain <= d) { const u=d ? remain/d:1; return [a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*u,0,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*u]; }
    remain -= d;
  }
  const p=path.at(-1); return [p[0],0,p[1]];
}
function faceAt(id,t,p) {
  const a=acts[id]||{}, walks=(a.walk||[]).filter(w=>t>=w.t0);
  if(walks.length){const w=walks.at(-1), path=w.path;let rem=Math.max(0,t-w.t0)*(w.speed||1);
    for(let i=1;i<path.length;i++){const d=Math.hypot(path[i][0]-path[i-1][0],path[i][1]-path[i-1][1]);if(rem<=d)return [path[i][0]-path[i-1][0],path[i][1]-path[i-1][1]];rem-=d;}
    const n=path.length;return [path[n-1][0]-path[n-2][0],path[n-1][1]-path[n-2][1]]; }
  const look=(a.look||[]).filter(k=>k.t<=t).sort((x,y)=>x.t-y.t).at(-1);
  if(look)return [look.at[0]-p[0],look.at[2]-p[2]];
  const ry=(cast[id].ry||0)*Math.PI/180;return [Math.sin(ry),Math.cos(ry)];
}
const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const lerp=(a,b,u)=>a+(b-a)*u;
function lerpV(a,b,u){return a.map((v,i)=>lerp(v,b[i],u));}

function add(a,b){return a.map((x,i)=>x+b[i]);}
function ry(v,yaw){const a=yaw*Math.PI/180;return [v[0]*Math.cos(a)+v[2]*Math.sin(a),v[1],-v[0]*Math.sin(a)+v[2]*Math.cos(a)];}
function box(center,size,yaw=0){const [x,y,z]=size,a=Math.abs(yaw*Math.PI/180);const ex=Math.abs(x*Math.cos(a))+Math.abs(z*Math.sin(a)),ez=Math.abs(x*Math.sin(a))+Math.abs(z*Math.cos(a));return {center,min:[center[0]-ex/2,center[1]-y/2,center[2]-ez/2],max:[center[0]+ex/2,center[1]+y/2,center[2]+ez/2],size:[ex,y,ez]};}
function corners(b){const out=[];for(const x of [b.min[0],b.max[0]])for(const y of [b.min[1],b.max[1]])for(const z of [b.min[2],b.max[2]])out.push([x,y,z]);return out;}
function makeFrame(cam){const f=norm(sub(cam.target,cam.pos)), right=norm(cross(f,[0,1,0])), up=norm(cross(right,f));const v=cam.fov*Math.PI/180,h=2*Math.atan(Math.tan(v/2)*1080/1920);const planes=[];
  for(const [axis,ang,other,swap] of [[right,h/2,up,false],[neg(right),h/2,up,true],[up,v/2,right,true],[neg(up),v/2,right,false]]){const edge=norm(add(f,mul(axis,Math.tan(ang))));let n=norm(swap?cross(other,edge):cross(edge,other));if(dot(n,f)<0)n=neg(n);planes.push({n});}
  planes.push({n:f,dot:0,origin:cam.pos,near:true},{n:neg(f),dot:0,origin:cam.pos,far:true});
  return {cam,planes,point(p){const d=sub(p,cam.pos);return dot(d,f)>=.1&&dot(d,f)<=2000&&planes.slice(0,4).every(pl=>dot(d,pl.n)>=-1e-7);},sphere(p,r){const d=sub(p,cam.pos);return dot(d,f)-r>=.1&&dot(d,f)+r<=2000&&planes.slice(0,4).every(pl=>dot(d,pl.n)>=r-1e-7);},depth(p){return dot(sub(p,cam.pos),f);},height(size,p){const d=this.depth(p);return d>0?2*Math.atan(size/(2*d))/(cam.fov*Math.PI/180)*1920:null;},width(size,p){const d=this.depth(p);return d>0?2*Math.atan(size/(2*d))/(h)*1080:null;},screen(p){const d=sub(p,cam.pos),z=dot(d,f);if(z<=.1)return null;const x=dot(d,right)/(z*Math.tan(h/2)),y=dot(d,up)/(z*Math.tan(v/2));return [(x+1)*540,(1-y)*960];}};
}
const sub=(a,b)=>a.map((v,i)=>v-b[i]), mul=(a,k)=>a.map(v=>v*k), dot=(a,b)=>a.reduce((s,v,i)=>s+v*b[i],0), norm=a=>mul(a,1/(Math.hypot(...a)||1)), cross=(a,b)=>[a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0]], neg=a=>mul(a,-1);
function segBox(p0,p1,b){let lo=0,hi=1;for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const d=p1[i]-p0[i];if(Math.abs(d)<1e-9){if(p0[i]<b.min[i]||p0[i]>b.max[i])return null;}else{let a=(b.min[i]-p0[i])/d,z=(b.max[i]-p0[i])/d;if(a>z)[a,z]=[z,a];lo=Math.max(lo,a);hi=Math.min(hi,z);if(lo>hi)return null;}}return lo>1e-6&&lo<1-1e-6?lo:null;}
function occluder(p0,p1,t,exclude){return occs(t).filter(o=>o.name!==exclude).map(o=>({name:o.name,k:segBox(p0,p1,o)})).filter(x=>x.k!=null).sort((a,b)=>a.k-b.k)[0]?.name||null;}

for(const x of [580,600,620])solids.push({name:`neighbour house @${x}`,min:[x-2.45,0,8-2.05],max:[x+2.45,5.6,8+2.05]});
function occs(t){const out=solids.map(b=>({...b}));out.push({name:'seawall',...box([0,9,-16],[90,18,1.15])});const g=props.find(p=>p.id==='gateleaf');if(g){const st=propState(g,t);if(st.visible)out.push({name:'gateleaf',...box(add(st.at,[0,17.88*(st.lift-.5),0]),[7,17.88,.36])});}return out;}
function propState(p,t){const ks=p.keys||[], before=ks.filter(k=>k.t<=t).sort((a,b)=>a.t-b.t), key=before.at(-1)||{};const lastField=f=>before.filter(k=>k[f]!=null).at(-1)?.[f];const visible=key.drop?true:(lastField('in')??p.in??'world')==='world';const at=[...(lastField('at')??p.at)],rot=[...(lastField('rot')??p.rot??[0,0,0])];
  const val=(field,def)=>{if(p[field]==null)return def;const keyed=before.filter(k=>k[field]!=null);if(!keyed.length)return p[field];const prev=keyed.at(-1),ix=keyed.length-1,ease=prev.ease;if(ease!=null&&ix>0){const prior=keyed[ix-1],u=clamp((t-prev.t)/ease);const sm=u*u*(3-2*u);return lerp(prior[field],prev[field],sm);}return prev[field];};
  return {visible,at,rot,page:val('page',0),h:val('h',.8),lift:val('lift',0),lineY:val('lineY',1),pin:val('pin',0),cx:val('cx',0),cy:val('cy',12)};}
function dropState(p,t){const k=(p.keys||[]).filter(k=>k.t<=t).at(-1);if(!k?.drop)return null;const d=k.drop,dt=t-k.t,g=d.g??9.8,v0=d.v0||[0,0,0],from=d.from||k.at||p.at,ground=d.ground??0,a=g/2,c=from[1]-ground,T=a>0?(v0[1]+Math.sqrt(v0[1]**2+4*a*Math.max(0,c)))/(2*a):Infinity;if(d.hide&&dt>T)return null;const u=Math.min(dt,T),y=from[1]+v0[1]*u-.5*g*u*u;return [from[0]+v0[0]*u,y,from[2]+v0[2]*u];}
function boxCheck(frame,b,minPx=0,skipOcc=false,name='subject'){const n=corners(b).filter(p=>frame.point(p)).length,center=frame.point(b.center),px=frame.height(b.size[1],b.center),pw=frame.width(b.size[0],b.center),by=!skipOcc?occluder(frame.cam.pos,b.center,currentTime,name):null,behind=frame.depth(b.center)<=0;return {pass:center&&!by&&px!=null&&px>=minPx&&(n>=4||px>=1920||pw>=1080),n,px,pw,center,occluded:by,behind};}
let currentTime=0;
function propBox(id,t){const p=props.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!p)return null;const s=propState(p,t);if(!s.visible)return null;const a=s.at,r=s.rot[1]||0,kind=p.kind;let B,points=[];
  if(kind==='boardsign')B=box(add(a,[0,(p.post?p.leg||1.2:0)+(p.h||.8)/2,0]),[p.w||2,p.h||.8,.12],r);
  if(kind==='calendar')B=box(add(a,[0,1.9,0]),[2.2,3.5,.2],r);
  if(kind==='measure'){if(id.endsWith('label'))return null;}
  if(kind==='money')B=box(add(a,[0,.55,0]),[3.7,1.15,2.1],r);
  if(kind==='crane')B=box(add(a,ry([2,8,0],r)),[11.5,16.8,1.2],r);
  if(kind==='gateleaf')B=box(add(a,[0,17.88*(s.lift-.5),0]),[7,17.88,.36],r);
  if(kind==='seawall')B=box(add(a,[0,9,0]),[90,18,1.15],r);
  if(kind==='blueprint')B=box(add(a,[0,1.7,0]),[3.4,3.4,.2],r);
  if(kind==='orb')return {sphere:add(a,[s.cx,s.cy,0]),r:p.r||1.5,state:s};
  if(kind==='coins'){const q=dropState(p,t);return q?{box:box(q,[.5,.5,.5]),state:s}:null;}
  return B?{box:B,state:s}:null;
}

function pointCheck(frame,p){return frame.point(p)&&!occluder(frame.cam.pos,p,currentTime);}
function screenVerticalSpan(frame, points){const ys=points.map(p=>frame.screen(p)).filter(p=>p!==null).map(p=>p[1]);return ys.length?Math.max(...ys)-Math.min(...ys):0;}
function faceCheck(frame,id,t,minPx=0){const p=posAt(id,t),head=[p[0],1.9,p[2]],f=faceAt(id,t,p),to=[frame.cam.pos[0]-p[0],frame.cam.pos[2]-p[2]],angle=Math.acos(clamp(dot(norm(f),norm(to)),-1,1))*180/Math.PI;const visible=frame.sphere(head,.5),occ=occluder(frame.cam.pos,head,t),px=frame.height(1,head);return {pass:angle<=60&&visible&&!occ&&px!=null&&px>=minPx,angle,visible,occ,px};}

/bin/bash -lc "sed -n '1,130p' engine/clips.js; rg -n 'watch_stand|point_accuse|carry_walk|run_panic|reach_touch_wall|bow_hold_flowers|stand_relief'" in /home/hatch/workspace/fudai-rebuild/studio/projects/fudai-wall
 succeeded in 763ms:
// engine/clips.js - one function per clip: clip(rest, params) -> { dur, ch: { 'path': track }, release: [...], events: [...] }.
// Times are clip-local seconds (F(n) = n frames). Values are CONTRACT Pose channels. Everything is relative to `rest`
// ({ hipY, shoulderR, shoulderL, hipR, hipL, armLen, legLen, headY }, body space: origin at the hem, +Y up, +Z facing,
// +X the character's LEFT), so a clip fits a kid or a tall man.
// Key values: '@' = the value underneath at the clip's start, { '@': d } = that + d. A path ending in '+' is additive.
// After `dur` a clip HOLDS its last values (holds are dead still, law 5) until a later clip writes the channel, except the
// channels in `release` (they fall back to what is underneath; face.* always releases).
// Sign conventions = engine/rig.js: body.bend + = torso tips forward (toward +Z), body.lean + = sideways toward +X (his left), body.twist +
// = toward his left; head.yaw + = toward his left; head.pitch + = nose UP. Hands 'body' = the hem frame (moves with bend); feet 'root' = ground frame. Prop rot in degrees (x, y, z), the
// sledge/cane swing plane is the body's Y-Z plane, handle angle a (0 = level forward, 90 = up) -> rot.x = -a.
import { F, clamp, lerp, fastIn, careful, E, hash } from './ease.js';
import { K, S, FROM } from './track.js';

const sgn = side => side === 'L' ? 1 : -1;            // R = -X
const sh = (r, side) => side === 'L' ? r.shoulderL : r.shoulderR;
const hp = (r, side) => side === 'L' ? r.hipL : r.hipR;
const ank = r => r.ankleY ?? 0;                        // foot targets are ankles: ankleY above the ground (rig rest)
// head centre above the HEM: the rig's rest.headY may be the height above the ground (character.js) or above the hem (brief)
export const hh = r => r.headY > r.hipY + .3 ? r.headY - r.hipY : r.headY;

/** Hand / foot targets in body space, all from rest. */
export const at = {
  hang: (r, s) => { const o = sh(r, s); return [o[0] * 1.15, o[1] - r.armLen * .737, .03]; },                  // = rig.js restPose for armLen .95
  hip: (r, s) => { const h = hp(r, s); return [h[0] + sgn(s) * .2, .1, h[2] + .02]; },                       // akimbo knuckles on the hip
  chest: (r, s) => { const o = sh(r, s); return [sgn(s) * .1, o[1] - .18, o[2] + .3]; },
  forward: (r, s) => { const o = sh(r, s); return [o[0] * .8, o[1] - .02, o[2] + r.armLen * .9]; },              // point / flick, arm level
  side: (r, s) => { const o = sh(r, s); return [o[0] + sgn(s) * r.armLen * .38, o[1] - r.armLen * .55, o[2] + .22]; },   // shrug: hands out low and small, palms-up feel (M2: was a T)
  up: (r, s) => { const o = sh(r, s); return [o[0] + sgn(s) * r.armLen * .45, o[1] + r.armLen * .75, o[2] + .05]; }, // panic \ /
  brim: (r, s) => [sgn(s) * .64, hh(r) + .14, .3],                                                           // M2: out of the head (the far hand hid behind it)
  temple: (r, s) => [sgn(s) * .6, hh(r) + .04, .1],
  mouth: (r) => [0, hh(r) - .38, .5],
  face: (r, s) => [sgn(s) * .28, hh(r) - .1, .55],                                                           // brace: hand up before the face
  foot: (r, s) => { const h = hp(r, s); return [h[0], ank(r), h[2]]; },                                       // a foot on the ground, ROOT space
};
const elbowOut = (s, back = -.3) => [sgn(s), 0, back];
const hangBend = s => [sgn(s) * .45, 0, -1];         // = rig.js restPose
const elbowDown = (s, out = .7, fwd = .4) => [sgn(s) * out, -.8, fwd];   // M2: hand up at the head, elbow out and DOWN (no loop over the head)

/** Dense per-frame keys from a function f(frame) -> value, frames 0..n (linear between frames = exact on ones). */
const dense = (n, f) => K(Array.from({ length: n + 1 }, (_, i) => [F(i), f(i)]), 'lin');

// ---- holds and whole-body ----

/** hold: exact zero motion (section 9 `hold`). Writes nothing: what is underneath stays bit-identical. */
export const hold = (r, { frames = 30 } = {}) => ({ dur: F(frames), ch: {} });
export const idle = hold;

/** stop: the body catches its momentum after a walk: lean +3 in 2 f, back to 0 by f8 (law 7: tiny, no rubber). */
export const stop = (r, { deg = 3 } = {}) => ({ dur: F(8),
  ch: { 'body.bend+': K([[0, 0], [F(2), deg, 'gesture'], [F(8), 0, 'fastIn']]) } });

/** turn in place: root.ry by `by` deg over 10 f (body speed, law 3); the feet step it: R f0-6, L f4-10. */
export function turn(r, { by = 90, frames = 10 } = {}) {
  const n = frames, lift = .14 * r.legLen;
  const ryAt = i => by * fastIn(i / n);
  const footStep = (s, a, b) => i => {
    const u = clamp((i / n - a) / (b - a)), lagDeg = by * E.inOutSine(u) - ryAt(i);   // foot heading minus body heading
    const h = hp(r, s), c = Math.cos(lagDeg * Math.PI / 180), si = Math.sin(lagDeg * Math.PI / 180);
    return [h[0] * c + h[2] * si, ank(r) + lift * Math.sin(Math.PI * u), -h[0] * si + h[2] * c];
  };
  return { dur: F(n), release: ['legR.foot', 'legR.in', 'legL.foot', 'legL.in'],
    ch: { 'root.ry+': dense(n, ryAt), 'legR.in': S([[0, 'root']]), 'legL.in': S([[0, 'root']]),
      'legR.foot': dense(n, footStep('R', 0, .6)), 'legL.foot': dense(n, footStep('L', .4, 1)) } };
}

const kneelH = r => r.legLen * .5;
const seatH = r => r.legLen * .42;

/** kneel: appears already done (section 3 "Sit, kneel": no transition shown). frames > 0 animates it (body speed, careful weight). */
export function kneel(r, { frames = 0, bend = 30 } = {}) {
  const T = F(frames), e = frames ? 'careful' : 'snap', hY = kneelH(r);
  return { dur: T, ch: {
    'body.hipY': K([[0, FROM], [T, hY, e]]), 'body.kneel': K([[0, FROM], [T, 1, e]]), 'body.squash': K([[0, FROM], [T, .85, e]]),
    'body.bend': K([[0, FROM], [T, bend, e]]),
    'legR.in': S([[0, 'root']]), 'legL.in': S([[0, 'root']]),
    'legR.foot': K([[0, FROM], [T, [hp(r, 'R')[0], ank(r), -.45], e]]),            // knee down, foot behind
    'legL.foot': K([[0, FROM], [T, [hp(r, 'L')[0], ank(r), .42], e]]),             // front foot flat
    'legR.bend': K([[0, [0, 0, 1]]]), 'legL.bend': K([[0, [0, 0, 1]]]) } };
}

/** Seated feet (root space) for a seat height (D, the seat surface = the hem height): knees forward, shins down. M2: the feet
 *  go further forward on a low seat (thigh ~ .45 legLen forward, shin to the ground), a touch apart; a high seat (> standing
 *  hip height * .9) dangles them under the knees. */
// FIX-SEAT: thigh .45 -> .58 legLen: the knees used to end AT the front edge of a bench sat on at its centre mark, so the lap, the knee
// and the shins hid behind the seat's front face; now the knees overhang it and the shins come down in front of it
const seatFeet = (r, seat, s) => { const h = hp(r, s), thigh = .58 * r.legLen, shin = Math.max(0, seat - ank(r)), dangle = seat > r.hipY * .9;
  return [h[0] * 1.3, dangle ? Math.max(ank(r), seat - r.legLen * .55) : ank(r), h[2] + thigh + (dangle ? 0 : Math.max(0, r.legLen * .5 - shin) * .6)]; };

/** FIX-SEAT (2026-11-04, blind3 Nora: no legs, body sunk into the bench): `seat` is the seat SURFACE. The hem used to go exactly
 *  there, so the hip sockets (below the hem) and the whole thigh line ran INSIDE the seat slab: the slab hid the legs and the lap
 *  from every camera above seat height (and the skirt drape folded under the seat). The hem now rests where the thighs lie ON the
 *  surface: socket drop + the leg's half width + 1.5 cm. Exported so compose (seated reach) uses the same number. */
export const seatHem = (r, seat) => seat + Math.max(0, -hp(r, 'R')[1]) + Math.max(0, ank(r) - .005) + .015;

/** sit: appears seated (section 3). seat = seat height above ground (D) = where the hem rests. */
export function sit(r, { seat = seatH(r), frames = 0 } = {}) {
  const T = F(frames), e = frames ? 'careful' : 'snap';
  return { dur: T, ch: {
    'body.hipY': K([[0, FROM], [T, seatHem(r, seat), e]]), 'body.sit': K([[0, FROM], [T, 1, e]]), 'body.bend': K([[0, FROM], [T, 0, e]]),
    'legR.in': S([[0, 'root']]), 'legL.in': S([[0, 'root']]),
    'legR.foot': K([[0, FROM], [T, seatFeet(r, seat, 'R'), e]]), 'legL.foot': K([[0, FROM], [T, seatFeet(r, seat, 'L'), e]]),
    'legR.bend': K([[0, [-.1, .6, 1]]]), 'legL.bend': K([[0, [.1, .6, 1]]]) } };
}

/** sit_down: not in his video (section 10); built from the laws: lean in 4 f (body), hips down 14 f careful (weight), lean back by f16.
 *  seat = seat height (D); the feet step forward to seatFeet while the hips go down. */
export function sit_down(r, { seat = seatH(r) } = {}) {
  return { dur: F(16), ch: {
    'body.bend': K([[0, FROM], [F(4), 18, 'fastIn'], [F(10), 14, 'lin'], [F(16), 0, 'fastIn']]),
    'body.hipY': K([[0, FROM], [F(2), FROM], [F(14), seatHem(r, seat), 'careful']]), 'body.sit': S([[0, 0], [F(12), 1]]),
    'legR.in': S([[0, 'root']]), 'legL.in': S([[0, 'root']]),
    'legR.foot': K([[0, at.foot(r, 'R')], [F(4), at.foot(r, 'R')], [F(14), seatFeet(r, seat, 'R'), 'careful']]),
    'legL.foot': K([[0, at.foot(r, 'L')], [F(4), at.foot(r, 'L')], [F(14), seatFeet(r, seat, 'L'), 'careful']]),
    'legR.bend': K([[0, [-.1, .6, 1]]]), 'legL.bend': K([[0, [.1, .6, 1]]]) } };
}

/** sit_back (FX1, the last step of a timeline `sit_on`): standing just in front of a seat, facing away from it, he sits BACK onto it with
 *  the feet planted: the root slides back `back` D (default = how far seated feet sit in front of the hips, so the feet end exactly at
 *  seatFeet) while the root-space feet move forward by the same eased amount, so in the world they never move (no slide). Lean 4 f,
 *  hips + root back f4-14 careful, lean back by f16 (= sit_down's timing). `ry` = the facing (deg) the back move goes against.
 *  Its root move is permanent (compose keeps a seat clip's root channels after a stand_up ends the seat). */
export function sit_back(r, { seat = seatH(r), back = null, ry = 0, shift = null } = {}) {

    'body.bend': K([[0, FROM], [F(4), 18, 'fastIn'], [F(10), 14, 'lin'], [F(16), 0, 'fastIn']]),

// lab/film/resolve.js: a film file -> the resolved scene the stage draws (2026-11-04, port job). Isomorphic: tools/make_film.mjs runs it in
// node (fs io + puppeteer stages), the app's host page (templates/host/chost.js) runs the SAME code in the page (fetch io + in-page stages).
// It is make_film's resolution moved here unchanged: voice words split into the script lines, every 'line:N...' time, per-character
// timelines (say / listen / blink / settle), hand-overs, sit_on, prop clips, collar tracks, listener gaze, rests, the film end, lowCams,
// autoMove (park drift, push pads, follow + parkAt split), and endFraming.
//
//   const R = await resolveFilm(film, io, opts)  -> { scene, dur, N, fps, W, H, voice, characters, rests, filmProps, END, snaps }
//   io = { log(...a), json(rel, from), text(rel, from), openStage(sceneDef) -> { info, frame(t) -> { views }, close() }, tts?(film),
//          checkVoice?(V), wavOf?(file), rel?(file) }
//        from: 'film' = the film folder (node) / the project root (app), 'root' = the engine root (charsys / the project), 'spec' = a cast
//        spec given as a .json path (charsys root / the project's scenes/)
//   opts = { name, scale (1; .5 = --preview), ownSet: { module, file, label } | null, probe, noDrift, noFacelimit, guardSnaps(characters, props, rests, fps) }
// The resolved.json make_film writes = { ...scene, dur, N, lines: voice.lines }.
import { evalPose } from '../../engine/compose.js';
